resolvedScope.periods.map(String) : [];\n  if (periods.length < 2) return null;\n  const fromPeriod = periods[0];\n  const toPeriod = periods[periods.length - 1];\n  let metrics = requestedMetrics_();\n  if (!metrics.length) metrics = availableTopMetrics.slice(0, 8);\n\n  const games = Array.isArray(resolvedScope.games) ? resolvedScope.games.map(String) : [];\n  const rows = [];\n\n  for (const game of games) {\n    for (const metric of metrics) {\n      const fromFact = factMap.get(factKey_(game, fromPeriod, '', metric));\n      const toFact = factMap.get(factKey_(game, toPeriod, '', metric));\n      if (!fromFact || !toFact) continue;\n      rows.push([\n        game,\n        metricLabel_(metric),\n        formatMetric_(metric, fromFact.value),\n        formatMetric_(metric, toFact.value),\n        change_(metric, fromFact.value, toFact.value)\n      ]);\n      if (rows.length >= 20) break;\n    }\n    if (rows.length >= 20) break;\n  }\n\n  if (!rows.length) return null;\n  return {\n    type: 'comparison_table',\n    title: `${fromPeriod} vs ${toPeriod}`,\n    columns: ['Game', 'Metric', fromPeriod, toPeriod, 'Change'],\n    rows\n  };\n}\n\nfunction buildChannelCompare_() {\n  if (!latestPeriod) return null;\n  const games = Array.isArray(resolvedScope.games) ? continue;\n    out.push((toFact.value - fromFact.value) * 100);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\nconst ppClaims = supportedPpClaims_();\n\nfunction numericClaimSupported_(claim) {\n  if (!claim || !Number.isFinite(Number(claim.number))) return false;\n  const number = Number(claim.number);\n\n  if (claim.suffix === 'pp') {\n    return ppClaims.some(value => near_(value, number, 0.011));\n  }\n\n  if (claim.suffix === '%') {\n    return facts.some(fact => {\n      if (isRateMetric_(fact.metric) && Math.abs(fact.value) <= 1.5) {\n        return near_(fact.value * 100, number, 0.011);\n      }\n      return near_(fact.value, number, 0.011);\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (metadataClaims.some(value => near_(value, number, 1e-9))) return true;\n  if (facts.some(fact => near_(fact.value, number, 1e-9))) return true;\n  return false;\n}\n\nfunction validateTextGrounding_(value) {\n  const text = cleanText_(value, 2200);\n  const claims = extractNumericClaims_(text);\n  const unsupported = claims.filter(claim => !numericClaimSupported_(claim));\n  const causeSupported = hasExplicitCausalEvidence_() || !hasStrongCauseClaim_(text);\n  return {\n    ok: unsupported.length === 0 && causeSupported,\n    unsupported_numbers: unsupported.map(item => item.raw),\n    unsupported_cause: !causeSupported\n  };\n}\n\nfunction metricMentioned_(metric, text) {\n  const value = String(text || '').toLowerCase();\n  const day = retentionDay_(metric);\n  if (day !== null) return new RegExp(`(^|[^a-z0-9])d${day}([^a-z0-9]|$)`, 'i').test(value);\n\n  const aliases = {\n    new_register: ['new register', 'register', 'ผู้สมัครใหม่', 'สมัครใหม่'],\n    register_users: ['register', 'ผู้สมัคร', 'ผู้สมัครใหม่'],\n    paid_register: ['paid register', 'paid', 'สมัครจากโฆษณา'],\n    first_login: ['first login', 'เข้าเกมครั้งแรก'],\n    first_login_users: ['first login', 'เข้าเกมครั้งแรก'],\n    returners: ['returner', 'ผู้เล่นกลับมา', 'กลับมาเล่น'],\n    late_starters: ['late starter', 'เข้าเกมช้า', 'เริ่มเล่นช้า'],\n    avg_days_active: ['avg active', 'active days', 'วัน active', 'วันเล่นเฉลี่ย', 'วันเล่น'],\n    avg_dau: ['avg dau', 'dau เฉลี่ย'],\n    max_dau: ['max dau', 'dau สูงสุด']\n  };\n  return (aliases[metric] || [String(metric).replace(/_/g, ' ')]).some(alias => value.includes(alias));\n}\n\nfunction gameMentioned_(game, text) {\n  const normalized = String(text || '').toUpperCase().replace(/[\\s-]+/g, '_');\n  return normalized.includes(String(game || '').toUpperCase().replace(/[\\s-]+/g, '_'));\n}\n\nfunction answerQualityV3_(answer) {\n  const text = cleanText_(answer, 2200);\n  const reasons = [];\n  const games = Array.isArray(resolvedScope.games) ? resolvedScope.games.map(String) : [];\n  const availableRetention = retentionMetrics.filter(metric => factsForMetric_(metric).length);\n\n  if (\n    text.length < 45 &&\n    ['summary', 'compare', 'diagnose', 'explain', 'evaluate', 'recommend', 'explore'].includes(questionMode)\n  ) reasons.push('answer_too_short');\n\n  const isPerformanceSummary = intent === 'performance_summary' || (\n    /performance/i.test(question) && /(สรุป|summary)/i.test(question)\n  );\n  const isRetentionQuestion = /retention/i.test(question) || /retention/i.test(intent);\n\n  if (isPerformanceSummary) {\n    const volumeMetrics = ['new_register', 'register_users', 'paid_register', 'avg_dau'].filter(m => availableTopMetrics.includes(m));\n    const engagementMetrics = ['returners', 'avg_days_active', 'late_starters'].filter(m => availableTopMetrics.includes(m));\n\n    const volumeCovered = volumeMetrics.some(metric => metricMentioned_(metric, text));\n    const engagementCovered = engagementMetrics.some(metric => metricMentioned_(metric, text));\n    const retentionMentionCount = availableRetention.filter(metric => metricMentioned_(metric, text)).length;\n\n    if (volumeMetrics.length && !volumeCovered) reasons.push('performance_missing_volume');\n    if (engagementMetrics.length && !engagementCovered) reasons.push('performance_missing_engagement');\n    if (availableRetention.length >= 2 && retentionMentionCount < Math.min(2, availableRetention.length)) {\n      reasons.push('performance_retention_curve_incomplete');\n    }\n    if (games.length >= 2 && games.filter(game => gameMentioned_(game, text)).length < 2) {\n      reasons.push('performance_missing_cross_game_insight');\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (isRetentionQuestion && availableRetention.length >= 2) {\n    const mentioned = availableRetention.filter(metric => metricMentioned_(metric, text)).length;\n    if (mentioned < Math.min(2, availableRetention.length)) reasons.push('retention_curve_incomplete');\n  }\n\n  return { ok: reasons.length === 0, reasons };\n}\n\nfunction buildOverviewTableV3_() {\n  const games = Array.isArray(resolvedScope.games) ? resolvedScope.games.map(String) : [];\n  if (!latestPeriod || !games.length) return null;\n\n  const nonRetentionPriority = [\n    'new_register', 'register_users', 'paid_register',\n    'returners', 'avg_days_active', 'avg_dau', 'max_dau'\n  ].filter(metric => availableTopMetrics.includes(metric));\n\n  // Frontend/Apps Script allow max 10 columns. Keep all retention milestones first.\n  const ret = retentionMetrics.filter(metric => factsForMetric_(metric).length);\n  const roomForMetrics = 9; // plus Game column = 10\n  const selected = [];\n  for (const metric of nonRetentionPriority) {\n    if (selected.length + ret.length >= roomForMetrics) break;\n    if (!selected.includes(metric)) selected.push(metric);\n  }\n  for (const metric of ret) {\n    if (selected.length >= roomForMetrics) break;\n    if (!selected.includes(metric)) selected.push(metric);\n  }\n\n  if (!selected.length) return null;\n  const rows = [];\n  for (const game of games) {\n    const cells = [game];\n    let hasAny = false;\n    for (const metric of selected) {\n      const fact = factMap.get(factKey_(game, latestPeriod, '', metric));\n      const value = fact ? formatMetric_(metric, fact.value) : '';\n      if (value) hasAny = true;\n      cells.push(value || '-');\n    }\n    if (hasAny) rows.push(cells);\n  }\n  if (!rows.length) return null;\n\n  return {\n    type: 'comparison_table',\n    title: `Performance Overview ${latestPeriod}`,\n    columns: ['Game', ...selected.map(metricLabel_)],\n    rows\n  };\n}\n\nfunction buildRetentionTableV3_() {\n  if (!latestPeriod || retentionMetrics.length < 2) return null;\n  const games = Array.isArray(resolvedScope.games) ? resolvedScope.games.map(String) : [];\n  const metrics = retentionMetrics.filter(metric => factsForMetric_(metric).length).slice(0, 9);\n  if (metrics.length < 2) return null;\n  const rows = [];\n  for (const game of games) {\n    const cells = [game];\n    let hasAny = false;\n    for (const metric of metrics) {\n      const fact = factMap.get(factKey_(game, latestPeriod, '', metric));\n      const value = fact ? formatMetric_(metric, fact.value) : '';\n      if (value) hasAny = true;\n      cells.push(value || '-');\n    }\n    if (hasAny) rows.push(cells);\n  }\n  if (!rows.length) return null;\n  return {\n    type: 'comparison_table',\n    title: `Retention Curve ${latestPeriod}`,\n    columns: ['Game', ...metrics.map(metricLabel_)],\n    rows\n  };\n}\n\nfunction requestedMetricsV3_() {\n  const metrics = Array.isArray(resolvedScope.metrics) ? resolvedScope.metrics.map(String) : [];\n  return metrics.filter(metric => availableTopMetrics.includes(metric));\n}\n\nfunction buildPeriodComparisonV3_() {\n  const periods = Array.isArray(resolvedScope.periods) ? resolvedScope.periods.map(String) : [];\n  if (periods.length < 2) return null;\n  const fromPeriod = periods[0];\n  const toPeriod = periods[periods.length - 1];\n  let metrics = requestedMetricsV3_();\n  if (!metrics.length) metrics = availableTopMetrics.slice(0, 5);\n\n  const games = Array.isArray(resolvedScope.games) ? resolvedScope.games.map(String) : [];\n  const rows = [];\n  for (const game of games) {\n    for (const metric of metrics) {\n      const fromFact = factMap.get(factKey_(game, fromPeriod, '', metric));\n      const toFact = factMap.get(factKey_(game, toPeriod, '', metric));\n      if (!fromFact || !toFact) continue;\n      const delta = change_(metric, fromFact.value, toFact.value);\n      rows.push([\n        game, metricLabel_(metric),\n        formatMetric_(metric, fromFact.value),\n        formatMetric_(metric, toFact.value),\n        delta ? delta.display : ''\n      ]);\n      if (rows.length >= 20) break;\n    }\n    if (rows.length >= 20) break;\n  }\n  if (!rows.length) return null;\n  return {\n    type: 'comparison_table',\n    title: `${fromPeriod} vs ${toPeriod}`,\n    columns: ['Game', 'Metric', fromPeriod, toPeriod, 'Change'],\n    rows\n  };\n}\n\nfunction buildGameComparisonV3_() {\n  if (!latestPeriod) return null;\n  let metrics = requestedMetricsV3_();\n  if (!metrics.length) metrics = retentionMetrics.length ? retentionMetrics : availableTopMetrics.slice(0, 5);\n  metrics = metrics.slice(0, 9);\n  if (!metrics.length) return null;\n\n  const games = Array.isArray(resolvedScope.games) ? resolvedScope.games.map(String) : [];\n  const rows = [];\n  for (const game of games) {\n    const row = [game];\n    let hasAny = false;\n    for (const metric of metrics) {\n      const fact = factMap.get(factKey_(game, latestPeriod, '', metric));\n      const value = fact ? formatMetric_(metric, fact.value) : '';\n      if (value) hasAny = true;\n      row.push(value || '-');\n    }\n    if (hasAny) rows.push(row);\n  }\n  if (!rows.length) return null;\n  return {\n    type: 'comparison_table',\n    title: `Game Comparison ${latestPeriod}`,\n    columns: ['Game', ...metrics.map(metricLabel_)],\n    rows\n  };\n}\n\nfunction buildChannelComparisonV3_() {\n  if (!latestPeriod) return null;\n  const games = Array.isArray(resolvedScope.games) ? resolvedScope.games.map(String) : [];\n  const game = games.length === 1 ? games[0].toUpperCase() : '';\n  if (!game) return null;\n\n  let metrics = Array.isArray(resolvedScope.metrics) ? resolvedScope.metrics.map(String) : [];\n  const channelFacts = facts.filter(f => f.game_code === game && f.period_key === latestPeriod && f.channel);\n  const available = [...new Set(channelFacts.map(f => f.metric))];\n  metrics = metrics.filter(m => available.includes(m));\n  if (!metrics.length) metrics = available.slice(0, 8);\n  metrics = metrics.slice(0, 8);\n  if (!metrics.length) return null;\n\n  const channels = [...new Set(channelFacts.map(f => f.channel))].sort();\n  const rows = [];\n  for (const channel of channels) {\n    const row = [channel];\n    let hasAny = false;\n    let exclude = false;\n    for (const metric of metrics) {\n      const fact = factMap.get(factKey_(game, latestPeriod, channel, metric));\n      if (fact && isRateMetric_(metric) && String(fact.quality_tier || '').toLowerCase() === 'insufficient_sample') {\n        exclude = true;\n      }\n      const value = fact ? formatMetric_(metric, fact.value) : '';\n      if (value) hasAny = true;\n      row.push(value || '-');\n    }\n    if (hasAny && !exclude) rows.push(row);\n  }\n  if (!rows.length) return null;\n  return {\n    type: 'comparison_table',\n    title: `Channel Comparison ${game} ${latestPeriod}`,\n    columns: ['Channel', ...metrics.map(metricLabel_)],\n    rows: rows.slice(0, 20)\n  };\n}\n\nfunction rankingDirection_(metric) {\n  const lowerBetter = isLowerBetterMetric_(metric);\n  if (/(ต่ำสุด|น้อยสุด|lowest|bottom)/i.test(question)) return 'asc';\n  if (/(สูงสุด|มากสุด|highest)/i.test(question)) return 'desc';\n  if (/(ดีที่สุด|ดีสุด|best|top)/i.test(question)) return lowerBetter ? 'asc' : 'desc';\n  if (/(แย่สุด|แย่ที่สุด|worst)/i.test(question)) return lowerBetter ? 'desc' : 'asc';\n  return 'desc';\n}\n\nfunction buildRankingV3_() {\n  let metrics = requestedMetricsV3_();\n  if (!metrics.length) {\n    metrics = availableTopMetrics.filter(metric => metricMentioned_(metric, question));\n  }\n  const metric = metrics[0];\n  if (!metric || !latestPeriod) return null;\n  const ranked = rankFacts_(metric, latestPeriod, rankingDirection_(metric));\n  if (!ranked.length) return null;\n  return {\n    type: 'ranking',\n    title: `${metricLabel_(metric)} Ranking ${latestPeriod}`,\n    items: ranked.slice(0, 10).map((fact, index) => ({\n      rank: index + 1,\n      label: fact.game_code,\n      metric: fact.metric,\n      value: formatMetric_(fact.metric, fact.value),\n      note: ''\n    }))\n  };\n}\n\nfunction buildMetricsV3_() {\n  let metrics = requestedMetricsV3_();\n  if (!metrics.length) metrics = availableTopMetrics.filter(metric => metricMentioned_(metric, question));\n  if (!metrics.length) return null;\n\n  const games = Array.isArray(resolvedScope.games) ? resolvedScope.games.map(String) : [];\n  const items = [];\n  for (const game of games) {\n    for (const metric of metrics) {\n      const fact = factMap.get(factKey_(game, latestPeriod, '', metric));\n      if (!fact) continue;\n      items.push({\n        label: game,\n        metric,\n        value: formatMetric_(metric, fact.value),\n        direction: 'neutral',\n        note: ''\n      });\n      if (items.length >= 12) break;\n    }\n    if (items.length >= 12) break;\n  }\n  if (!items.length) return null;\n  return { type: 'metrics', title: 'Metrics', items };\n}\n\nfunction buildSupportiveBlocksV3_(answer, dataRequirement) {\n  const blocks = [{\n    type: 'summary',\n    title: 'สรุป',\n    content: cleanText_(answer, 1200)\n  }];\n\n  if (dataRequirement === 'unsupported_data') {\n    blocks.push({\n      type: 'limitations',\n      title: 'ข้อจำกัดของข้อมูล',\n      items: [{\n        label: 'ยังยืนยันสาเหตุไม่ได้',\n        note: 'ข้อมูลที่มีอยู่รองรับการอธิบายสิ่งที่เกิดขึ้น แต่ยังไม่รองรับการยืนยันสาเหตุโดยตรง'\n      }]\n    });\n    return blocks;\n  }\n\n  const isPerformanceSummary = intent === 'performance_summary' || (\n    /performance/i.test(question) && /(สรุป|summary)/i.test(question)\n  );\n  const isRetention = /retention/i.test(intent) || /retention/i.test(question);\n\n  if (isPerformanceSummary) {\n    const table = buildOverviewTableV3_();\n    if (table) blocks.push(table);\n    return blocks;\n  }\n\n  if (comparisonMode === 'period_over_period') {\n    const table = buildPeriodComparisonV3_();\n    if (table) blocks.push(table);\n    return blocks;\n  }\n\n  if (comparisonMode === 'channel_over_channel') {\n    const table = buildChannelComparisonV3_();\n    if (table) blocks.push(table);\n    return blocks;\n  }\n\n  if (questionMode === 'rank' || /อันดับ|สูงสุด|ต่ำสุด|ดีที่สุด|แย่สุด|rank|best|worst/i.test(question)) {\n    const ranking = buildRankingV3_();\n    if (ranking) blocks.push(ranking);\n    return blocks;\n  }\n\n  if (comparisonMode === 'game_over_game' || /เปรียบเทียบ|compare/i.test(question)) {\n    const table = isRetention ? buildRetentionTableV3_() : buildGameComparisonV3_();\n    if (table) blocks.push(table);\n    return blocks;\n  }\n\n  if (isRetention) {\n    const table = buildRetentionTableV3_();\n    if (table) blocks.push(table);\n    return blocks;\n  }\n\n  if (questionMode === 'lookup') {\n    const metrics = buildMetricsV3_();\n    if (metrics) blocks.push(metrics);\n  }\n\n  return blocks;\n}\n\nfunction buildPerformanceFallbackV3_() {\n  const games = Array.isArray(resolvedScope.games) ? resolvedScope.games.map(String) : [];\n  if (!latestPeriod || !games.length) return '';\n\n  const lines = [];\n  const newRegisterMetric = availableTopMetrics.includes('new_register')\n    ? 'new_register'\n    : availableTopMetrics.includes('register_users') ? 'register_users' : '';\n  if (newRegisterMetric) {\n    const ranked = rankFacts_(newRegisterMetric, latestPeriod, 'desc');\n    if (ranked.length) {\n      lines.push(`ภาพรวม ${latestPeriod}: ${ranked[0].game_code} มี ${metricLabel_(newRegisterMetric)} สูงสุดที่ ${formatMetric_(newRegisterMetric, ranked[0].value)}`);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const engagementMetric = availableTopMetrics.includes('returners')\n    ? 'returners'\n    : availableTopMetrics.includes('avg_days_active') ? 'avg_days_active'\n    : availableTopMetrics.includes('avg_dau') ? 'avg_dau' : '';\n  if (engagementMetric) {\n    const ranked = rankFacts_(engagementMetric, latestPeriod, 'desc');\n    if (ranked.length) {\n      lines.push(`${ranked[0].game_code} เด่นด้าน ${metricLabel_(engagementMetric)} ที่ ${formatMetric_(engagementMetric, ranked[0].value)}`);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const ret = retentionMetrics.filter(metric => factsForMetric_(metric).length);\n  if (ret.length >= 2) {\n    const curveParts = [];\n    for (const game of games.slice(0, 4)) {\n      const values = [];\n      for (const metric of ret) {\n        const fact = factMap.get(factKey_(game, latestPeriod, '', metric));\n        if (fact) values.push(`${metricLabel_(metric)} ${formatMetric_(metric, fact.value)}`);\n      }\n      if (values.length >= 2) curveParts.push(`${game}: ${values.join(' → ')}`);\n    }\n    if (curveParts.length) lines.push(`Retention Curve — ${curveParts.join(' | ')}`);\n\n    const earlyMetric = ret[0];\n    const lateMetric = ret[ret.length - 1];\n    const earlyLow = rankFacts_(earlyMetric, latestPeriod, 'asc')[0];\n    const lateLow = rankFacts_(lateMetric, latestPeriod, 'asc')[0];\n    if (earlyLow && lateLow) {\n      if (earlyLow.game_code === lateLow.game_code) {\n        lines.push(`จุดที่ควรจับตาคือ ${earlyLow.game_code} ซึ่งต่ำสุดทั้ง ${metricLabel_(earlyMetric)} และ ${metricLabel_(lateMetric)} ในกลุ่ม`);\n      } else {\n        lines.push(`จุดที่ควรจับตา: ${earlyLow.game_code} ต่ำสุดที่ ${metricLabel_(earlyMetric)} ขณะที่ ${lateLow.game_code} ต่ำสุดที่ ${metricLabel_(lateMetric)}`);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return lines.join(' โดย ').slice(0, 1900);\n}\n\nfunction buildRetentionFallbackV3_() {\n  if (!latestPeriod || retentionMetrics.length < 2) return '';\n  const games = Array.isArray(resolvedScope.games) ? resolvedScope.games.map(String) : [];\n  const parts = [];\n  for (const game of games.slice(0, 6)) {\n    const values = [];\n    for (const metric of retentionMetrics) {\n      const fact = factMap.get(factKey_(game, latestPeriod, '', metric));\n      if (fact) values.push(`${metricLabel_(metric)} ${formatMetric_(metric, fact.value)}`);\n    }\n    if (values.length >= 2) parts.push(`${game}: ${values.join(' → ')}`);\n  }\n  if (!parts.length) return '';\n  const late = retentionMetrics[retentionMetrics.length - 1];\n  const high = rankFacts_(late, latestPeriod, 'desc')[0];\n  const low = rankFacts_(late, latestPeriod, 'asc')[0];\n  let suffix = '';\n  if (high && low && high.game_code !== low.game_code) {\n    suffix = ` โดย ${high.game_code} สูงสุดที่ ${metricLabel_(late)} ${formatMetric_(late, high.value)} และ ${low.game_code} ต่ำสุดที่ ${formatMetric_(late, low.value)}`;\n  }\n  return (`Retention ${latestPeriod}: ${parts.join(' | ')}` + suffix).slice(0, 1900);\n}\n\nfunction buildGenericFallbackV3_(dataRequirement) {\n  if (dataRequirement === 'unsupported_data') {\n    return 'จากข้อมูลที่มี สามารถยืนยันสิ่งที่เกิดขึ้นใน Performance ได้ แต่ยังไม่มีหลักฐานเพียงพอสำหรับยืนยันสาเหตุโดยตรง';\n  }\n\n  const isPerformanceSummary = intent === 'performance_summary' || (\n    /performance/i.test(question) && /(สรุป|summary)/i.test(question)\n  );\n  if (isPerformanceSummary) return buildPerformanceFallbackV3_();\n\n  if (/retention/i.test(intent) || /retention/i.test(question)) {\n    const retention = buildRetentionFallbackV3_();\n    if (retention) return retention;\n  }\n\n  const ranking = buildRankingV3_();\n  if (ranking && ranking.items.length) {\n    const top = ranking.items[0];\n    return `${top.label} อยู่ลำดับ 1 สำหรับ ${metricLabel_(top.metric)} ที่ ${top.value} ใน ${latestPeriod}`;\n  }\n\n  const metrics = buildMetricsV3_();\n  if (metrics && metrics.items.length) {\n    return metrics.items.slice(0, 6)\n      .map(item => `${item.label} ${metricLabel_(item.metric)} ${item.value}`)\n      .join(' • ');\n  }\n\n  return '';\n}\n\nfunction classifyTransportError_(issue) {\n  let raw = '';\n  try { raw = typeof issue === 'string' ? issue : JSON.stringify(issue); }\n  catch (_) { raw = String(issue || ''); }\n  const lower = raw.toLowerCase();\n\n  const out = {\n    error_code: 'gemini_request_failed',\n    retryable: false,\n    message: cleanText_(raw || 'Gemini request failed', 1200),\n    quota: {}\n  };\n\n  if (\n    lower.includes('generate_content_free_tier_requests') ||\n    lower.includes('quota exceeded') ||\n    lower.includes('resource_exhausted') ||\n    lower.includes('too many requests') ||\n    lower.includes('rate limit') ||\n    lower.includes('\"code\":429')\n  ) {\n    out.error_code = 'gemini_quota_exhausted';\n    out.message = 'Gemini quota or rate limit was exhausted. Automatic retry is disabled.';\n    out.quota = { exhausted: true };\n    return out;\n  }\n\n  if (lower.includes('invalid argument') || lower.includes('bad request') || lower.includes('\"code\":400')) {\n    out.error_code = 'gemini_bad_request';\n    out.message = cleanText_(raw || 'Gemini rejected the request.', 1200);\n    return out;\n  }\n\n  if (lower.includes('timeout') || lower.includes('timed out')) {\n    out.error_code = 'gemini_timeout';\n    out.message = 'Gemini request timed out. Automatic retry is disabled.';\n    return out;\n  }\n\n  return out;\n}\n\nconst {\n  gemini_prompt,\n  gemini_request,\n  compact_context,\n  ...publicBase\n} = base;\n\nlet answer = '';\nlet questionMode = '';\nlet dataRequirement = '';\nlet responseStyle = '';\nlet answerBlocks = [];\nlet error = '';\nlet errorCode = '';\nlet retryable = false;\nlet quota = {};\n\nconst validation = {\n  contract_version: 'conversational_v3_answer_first',\n  finish_reason: '',\n  structured_json: false,\n  metadata_valid: false,\n  answer_numeric_grounding: true,\n  answer_causal_grounding: true,\n  semantic_quality_pass: true,\n  semantic_quality_reasons: [],\n  answer_rebuilt: false,\n  answer_rebuild_reason: '',\n  built_answer_blocks: 0\n};\n\ntry {\n  const transportIssue =\n    (response && response.error) ||\n    (envelope && envelope.error) ||\n    ((Number(envelope.statusCode || 0) >= 400) ? envelope : null);\n\n  if (transportIssue) {\n    const classified = classifyTransportError_(transportIssue);\n    error = classified.message;\n    errorCode = classified.error_code;\n    retryable = false;\n    quota = classified.quota || {};\n  } else {\n    const candidate = Array.isArray(response.candidates) ? response.candidates[0] : null;\n    if (!candidate) {\n      errorCode = 'gemini_empty_candidate';\n      throw new Error('Gemini returned no candidate.');\n    }\n\n    const finishReason = String(candidate.finishReason || '').toUpperCase();\n    validation.finish_reason = finishReason;\n\n    if (finishReason === 'MAX_TOKENS') {\n      errorCode = 'gemini_output_truncated';\n      throw new Error('Gemini output was truncated by MAX_TOKENS. Automatic retry is disabled.');\n    }\n    if (finishReason === 'SAFETY') {\n      errorCode = 'gemini_safety_blocked';\n      throw new Error('Gemini response was blocked by safety filtering.');\n    }\n    if (finishReason && finishReason !== 'STOP') {\n      errorCode = 'gemini_incomplete_response';\n      throw new Error(`Gemini finished with ${finishReason}.`);\n    }\n\n    const text = (parseError) {\n      errorCode = 'gemini_invalid_json';\n      throw new Error(`Gemini returned invalid JSON: ${parseError.message}`);\n    }\n\n    questionMode = !grounding.unsupported_cause;\n\n    const quality = answerQualityV3_(answer);\n    validation.semantic_quality_pass = quality.ok;\n    validation.semantic_quality_reasons = quality.reasons;\n\n    if (!grounding.ok || !quality.ok) {\n      const rebuilt = buildGenericFallbackV3_(dataRequirement);\n      if (rebuilt) {\n        answer = rebuilt;\n        validation.answer_rebuilt = true;\n        validation.answer_rebuild_reason = !grounding.ok\n          ? (grounding.unsupported_cause ? 'unsupported_causal_claim' : 'unsupported_numeric_claim')\n          : 'semantic_quality_gate';\n      } else if (!grounding.ok) {\n        errorCode = grounding.unsupported_cause ? 'causal_grounding_failed' : 'numeric_grounding_failed';\n        throw new Error('Gemini answer contains unsupported grounded claims and no verified fallback could be built.');\n      }\n    }\n\n    answerBlocks = buildSupportiveBlocksV3_(answer, dataRequirement).slice(0, 12);\n    validation.built_answer_blocks = answerBlocks.length;\n  }\n} catch (issue) {\n  if (!error) error = cleanText_(issue && issue.message || issue || 'Gemini response normalization failed', 1200);\n  if (!errorCode) errorCode = 'gemini_structured_validation_failed';\n  retryable = false;\n}\n\nconst success = Boolean(\n  answer &&\n  !error &&\n  validation.structured_json &&\n  validation.metadata_valid\n);\n\nreturn [{ json: {\n  ...publicBase,\n  status: success ?
